dir=\"auto\"\u003eEasyVersionBackup is a lightweight Windows tool for creating versioned backups of folders.\nBack up save-game folders, office-document folders, Visual C# project folders, and other local directories.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eIt supports manual and automatic backups, multiple source → target configurations, ZIP backups, per-entry exclusions, skipped-file editor\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eConfirmation prompt before removing existing exclusion entries\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSupports source-relative paths and glob-style patterns\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePattern behavior:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e.sav\u003c/code\u003e and \u003ccode\u003e*.sav\u003c/code\u003e match \u003ccode\u003e.sav\u003c/code\u003e files\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e*\u003c/code\u003e matches characters within one path level\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003e**\u003c/code\u003e matches any number of folder levels\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eA trailing \u003ccode\u003e\\\u003c/code\u003e restricts the rule to folders\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eMatching is case-insensitive\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWildcard-only exclusions are rejected for safety\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv confirmed retention candidates\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDestructive actions are clearly highlighted\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv deleted\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCleanup does not run if the backup fails or is canceled\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 [!WARNING]","\u003e This software can permanently delete source and destination data.","\u003e Read the [Data-Loss Warning and Disclaimer](DISCLAIMER.md) before use.","","","## Important Safety Notice","","Retention and Source Cleanup are experimental features that can permanently delete source files or older backups.","","Both features are disabled by default and must be enabled explicitly. Always keep an independent, verified backup before using them.","","See the [Wiki](https://github.com/UncleRiot/EasyVersionBackup/wiki) for detailed configuration, restore instructions, limitations, and safety information.","","\u003cbr\u003e","\u003cbr\u003e","","","# EasyVersionBackup","","EasyVersionBackup is a lightweight Windows tool for creating versioned backups of folders. ","Back up save-game folders, office-document folders, Visual C# project folders, and other local directories.","","It supports manual and automatic backups, multiple source → target configurations, ZIP backups, per-entry exclusions, skipped-file reporting, system tray integration, and a clean WinForms interface.","","\u003cbr\u003e","","---","","\u003cbr\u003e","","## ❤️ Support","","EasyVersionBackup is free to use.","If this tool saves you time, you can support development here:","","\u003ca href=\"https://ko-fi.com/uncleriot\"\u003e","  \u003cimg src=\"https://storage.ko-fi.com/cdn/kofi2.png?v=3\" height=\"36\" alt=\"Support EasyVersionBackup on Ko-fi\" /\u003e","\u003c/a\u003e","","\u003cbr\u003e","\u003cbr\u003e","","---","","\u003cbr\u003e","\u003cbr\u003e","","","\u003e [!IMPORTANT]","\u003e If Windows SmartScreen blocks the app, right-click the EXE → **Properties** → check **Unblock** → **Apply** → **OK**, then start it again.","\u003e","","\u003cbr\u003e","","---","","","Main-Window","","\u003cbr\u003e","\u003cimg width=\"956\" height=\"326\" alt=\"grafik\" src=\"https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/fa575d05-ad64-47d0-b571-23e36ca8c9a0\" /\u003e","\u003cbr\u003e","","","Backup Pair Settings","","\u003cbr\u003e","\u003cimg width=\"420\" height=\"545\" alt=\"grafik\" src=\"https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/1c40f1c8-8850-43b3-942b-96adca495d70\" /\u003e","\u003cbr\u003e","","","Source Cleanup","","\u003cbr\u003e","\u003cimg width=\"620\" height=\"470\" alt=\"grafik\" src=\"https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/5d085162-6e20-4bb3-a952-b72d406892bd\" /\u003e","\u003cbr\u003e","","\u003cbr\u003e","\u003cimg width=\"520\" height=\"353\" alt=\"grafik\" src=\"https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/0eb99537-3643-44b5-a4f5-a3ecc3d5efd3\" /\u003e","\u003cbr\u003e","","\u003cbr\u003e","\u003cimg width=\"760\" height=\"430\" alt=\"grafik\" src=\"https://github.com/user-attachments/assets/caee9f1d-9609-41fb-b16b-bf744374f555\" /\u003e","\u003cbr\u003e","","","\u003cbr\u003e","\u003cbr\u003e","\u003cbr\u003e","","---","","","","## 🚀 Features","","### Versioned Backups","","- Create structured backups with version numbers","- Supports manual input, auto-increment, and timestamp-based versions","- Remembers manually adjusted versions per source → target pair","- Detects existing versions and suggests the next logical version","- Updated versions are stored only after a successful backup","- Canceling a backup does not advance or persist the version number","","### Multiple Backup Configurations","","- Configure multiple source → target pairs","- Enable or disable each entry individually","- Edit source and target paths directly in the main window","- Browse folders directly from the main table","- Configure versioning, retention, source cleanup, timers, and error handling per backup pair","","### Per-Entry Exclusions","","- Define excluded files or folders per backup entry","- Edit exclusions directly from the main window or Settings","- Add and remove exclusions through a table-based editor","- Confirmation prompt before removing existing exclusion entries","- Supports source-relative paths and glob-style patterns","","","Pattern behavior:","","- `.sav` and `*.sav` match `.sav` files","- `*` matches characters within one path level","- `**` matches any number of folder levels","- A trailing `\\` restricts the rule to folders","- Matching is case-insensitive","- Wildcard-only exclusions are rejected for safety","","### Backup Retention","","- Configure retention separately for every backup pair","- Keep the newest number of backups","- Keep backups for a configured number of days","- Combine retention rules using `AND` or `OR`","- Protect tagged backups such as `Final` or `OK`","- Preview affected backups before permanent deletion","- Choose between:","  - Canceling the complete backup","  - Continuing without deleting old backups","  - Creating the backup and deleting confirmed retention candidates","- Destructive actions are clearly highlighted","","### Source Cleanup","","- Experimental feature for removing old source files after a successful backup","- Must be enabled globally and per backup pair","- Supports multiple rules, for example:","  ```text","  .sav","  G1R*.sav","  Saved\\SaveGames\\.sav","  Saved\\SaveGames\\G1R*.sav","  ```","- Keep the newest files or delete files older than a selected date","- Shows a deletion preview before cleanup","- Directories are never deleted","- Cleanup does not run if the backup fails or is canceled","","### Flexible Backup Timer","","- Define intervals like `30s`, `5m`, or `1h`","- Configure a global timer or a custom timer per backup pair","- Live countdown visible:","  - In the main UI","  - In the window title","  - In the system tray tooltip","","### Silent Backup Progress","","- Backup progress is displayed directly inside the main table","- Source-related operations fill the source-directory cell","- Target-related operations fill the target-directory cell","- The timer column temporarily shows the current percentage","- Indeterminate phases use a subtle moving progress fill","- The normal timer value is restored after completion","- Multiple backup pairs can show progress independently","- No additional progress window or focus change is required","","### Automatic Background Backups","","- File and ZIP creation runs outside the UI thread","- Works while you use other applications","- Automatic backups remain silent unless user interaction is required","- Destination-conflict and retention confirmations can still require interaction","- Optional minimize-to-system-tray behavior","","### Status \u0026 Feedback System","","- Per-entry status indicator:","  - 🟢 OK","  - 🟡 Warning","  - 🔴 Error","- Hover for quick details:","  - Last backup time","  - Error messages","  - Skipped-file summary","- Click the info icon to open a copyable and scrollable details dialog","- Skipped files are listed individually when available","- Skipped files do not automatically mark a successful backup as failed","- Retention is indicated by a red `R` marker","- Source cleanup is indicated by a cyan `C` marker","- Both markers are displayed when both features are active","","### ZIP Support","","- Optional compression into `.zip` archives","- Uses the same versioning logic as folder-based backups","- Temporary ZIP files are finalized only after successful creation","- No additional metadata sidecar files are created","","### Robust Error Handling","","- Option to ignore locked or in-use files","- Backup can continue without interruption","- Skipped files are reported per backup entry","- Skipped-file summaries distinguish between files and folders","- Failed or canceled backups do not trigger source cleanup","- Useful for projects with temporary build files, IDE locks, or active services","","### System Tray Integration","","- Minimize to tray","- Tray tooltip shows the next scheduled backup:","","    Next backup in 2 minutes (19:52)","","- Manual and automatic backups can show tray notifications","- Canceled backups are reported as canceled instead of completed","","### Modernized UI","","- Clean, compact main window","- Toolbar with quick actions:","  - Exit","  - Add path","  - Remove path","  - Settings","  - About","  - Backup","- Modern themed dialogs and scrollbars","- Clear visual distinction between normal and destructive actions","- Main window initially shows only a small number of rows","- Window remains resizable","- Resize indicator included","- Tooltips and contextual help added for important controls","","---","","## 🧩 How It Works","","1. Configure one or more **source → target** paths","2. Optionally define exclusions per backup pair","3. Choose a versioning method","4. Optionally configure:","   - Retention","   - Source cleanup","   - Custom backup timer","   - Error handling","5. Click **Backup** or let the configured timer start it automatically","6. The app will:","   - Validate the source and target paths","   - Create a versioned directory or ZIP archive","   - Report progress directly in the backup-pair row","   - Skip problematic files if configured","   - Preview Source Cleanup and Retention candidates before deletion","   - Allow canceling the backup or continuing without deletion","   - Apply confirmed Source Cleanup only after a successful backup","   - Apply confirmed Retention after backup creation","   - Store the final backup status per entry","   - Persist the selected version only after success","","---","","## ⚙️ Settings Overview","","### Backup Timer","","Flexible input:","","- `30s` → 30 seconds","- `5m` → 5 minutes","- `1h` → 1 hour","- `15` → 15 minutes by default","","### Default Versioning","","Starting version or pattern, for example:","","- `none`","- `v1.0`","- `1.0`","- `yyyy-MM-dd`","- `yyyyMMdd`","- `yyyy-MM-dd-HH-mm`","- `yyyyMMddHHmm`","","### Auto Increment","","- Automatically increases compatible version numbers","- Updated versions are saved only after a successful backup","","### Retention","","- Keep the latest configured number of backups","- Keep backups for a configured number of days","- Combine enabled rules with `AND` or `OR`","- Exclude tagged backups from deletion","","### Source Cleanup","","- Delete only explicitly configured file types","- Restrict cleanup to specific source-relative folders","- Keep the newest files or delete files older than a selected date","","### Minimize to Systray","","- Keeps the app running quietly in the background","","### Ignore Copy Errors","","- Skips locked or problematic files instead of stopping the backup","","### ZIP Backups","","- Enable to create one `.zip` archive per backup","- Disable to create a versioned backup directory","","### Per-Path Exclusions","","- Exclude folders or files from individual backup entries","- Supports simple names, file extensions, relative paths, `*`, and `**`","","---","","## 🖥️ Runtime Requirement","","The published application targets Windows x64 and requires the .NET 8 Desktop Runtime because the project is framework-dependent (`SelfContained=false`).","","Application settings are stored in:","","    \u003capplication directory\u003e\\Settings\\EasyVersionBackup.settings.json","","The application no longer creates `.evbmeta` sidecar files beside ZIP backups.","","---","","## 💡 Use Cases","","- Backup Visual Studio projects","- Create safe snapshots before refactoring","- Preserve game saves while automatically removing older save files","- Keep only a defined number of recent backups","- Protect release backups using retention tags","- Save versions before deployments or releases","- Run automatic backups while working"],"stylingDirectives":null,"colorizedLines":["\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-ent\"\u003e\u0026gt; \u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Important Safety Notice</h2><a id="user-content-important-safety-notice" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Important Safety Notice" href="#important-safety-notice"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Retention and Source Cleanup are experimental features that can permanently delete source files or older backups.</p>
<p dir="auto">Both features are disabled by default and must be enabled explicitly. Always keep an independent, verified backup before using them.</p>
<p dir="auto">See the <a href="https://github.com/UncleRiot/EasyVersionBackup/wiki">Wiki</a> for detailed configuration, restore instructions, limitations, and safety information.</p>
<br>
<br>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">EasyVersionBackup</h1><a id="user-content-easyversionbackup" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: EasyVersionBackup" href="#easyversionbackup"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">EasyVersionBackup is a lightweight Windows tool for creating versioned backups of folders.
Back up save-game folders, office-document folders, Visual C# project folders, and other local directories.</p>
<p dir="auto">It supports manual and automatic backups, multiple source → target configurations, ZIP backups, per-entry exclusions, skipped-file reporting, system tray integration, and a clean WinForms interface.</p>
<br>
<hr>
<br>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">❤️ Support</h2><a id="user-content-️-support" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ❤️ Support" href="#️-support"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">EasyVersionBackup is free to use.
If this tool saves you time, you can support development here:</p>
<a href="https://ko-fi.com/uncleriot" rel="nofollow">

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🚀 Features</h2><a id="user-content--features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🚀 Features" href="#-features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Versioned Backups</h3><a id="user-content-versioned-backups" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Versioned Backups" href="#versioned-backups"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Create structured backups with version numbers</li>
<li>Supports manual input, auto-increment, and timestamp-based versions</li>
<li>Remembers manually adjusted versions per source → target pair</li>
<li>Detects existing versions and suggests the next logical version</li>
<li>Updated versions are stored only after a successful backup</li>
<li>Canceling a backup does not advance or persist the version number</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Multiple Backup Configurations</h3><a id="user-content-multiple-backup-configurations" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Multiple Backup Configurations" href="#multiple-backup-configurations"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Configure multiple source → target pairs</li>
<li>Enable or disable each entry individually</li>
<li>Edit source and target paths directly in the main window</li>
<li>Browse folders directly from the main table</li>
<li>Configure versioning, retention, source cleanup, timers, and error handling per backup pair</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Per-Entry Exclusions</h3><a id="user-content-per-entry-exclusions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Per-Entry Exclusions" href="#per-entry-exclusions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Define excluded files or folders per backup entry</li>
<li>Edit exclusions directly from the main window or Settings</li>
<li>Add and remove exclusions through a table-based editor</li>
<li>Confirmation prompt before removing existing exclusion entries</li>
<li>Supports source-relative paths and glob-style patterns</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Pattern behavior:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>.sav</code> and <code>*.sav</code> match <code>.sav</code> files</li>
<li><code>*</code> matches characters within one path level</li>
<li><code>**</code> matches any number of folder levels</li>
<li>A trailing <code>\</code> restricts the rule to folders</li>
<li>Matching is case-insensitive</li>
<li>Wildcard-only exclusions are rejected for safety</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Backup Retention</h3><a id="user-content-backup-retention" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Backup Retention" href="#backup-retention"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Configure retention separately for every backup pair</li>
<li>Keep the newest number of backups</li>
<li>Keep backups for a configured number of days</li>
<li>Combine retention rules using <code>AND</code> or <code>OR</code></li>
<li>Protect tagged backups such as <code>Final</code> or <code>OK</code></li>
<li>Preview affected backups before permanent deletion</li>
<li>Choose between:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Canceling the complete backup</li>
<li>Continuing without deleting old backups</li>
<li>Creating the backup and deleting confirmed retention candidates</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Destructive actions are clearly highlighted</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Source Cleanup</h3><a id="user-content-source-cleanup" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Source Cleanup" href="#source-cleanup"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Experimental feature for removing old source files after a successful backup</li>
<li>Must be enabled globally and per backup pair</li>
<li>Supports multiple rules, for example:
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=".sav
G1R*.sav
Saved\SaveGames\.sav
Saved\SaveGames\G1R*.sav"><pre lang="text" class="notranslate"><code>.sav
G1R*.sav
Saved\SaveGames\.sav
Saved\SaveGames\G1R*.sav
</code></pre></div>
</li>
<li>Keep the newest files or delete files older than a selected date</li>
<li>Shows a deletion preview before cleanup</li>
<li>Directories are never deleted</li>
<li>Cleanup does not run if the backup fails or is canceled</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Flexible Backup Timer</h3><a id="user-content-flexible-backup-timer" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Flexible Backup Timer" href="#flexible-backup-timer"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Define intervals like <code>30s</code>, <code>5m</code>, or <code>1h</code></li>
<li>Configure a global timer or a custom timer per backup pair</li>
<li>Live countdown visible:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>In the main UI</li>
<li>In the window title</li>
<li>In the system tray tooltip</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Silent Backup Progress</h3><a id="user-content-silent-backup-progress" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Silent Backup Progress" href="#silent-backup-progress"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Backup progress is displayed directly inside the main table</li>
<li>Source-related operations fill the source-directory cell</li>
<li>Target-related operations fill the target-directory cell</li>
<li>The timer column temporarily shows the current percentage</li>
<li>Indeterminate phases use a subtle moving progress fill</li>
<li>The normal timer value is restored after completion</li>
<li>Multiple backup pairs can show progress independently</li>
<li>No additional progress window or focus change is required</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Automatic Background Backups</h3><a id="user-content-automatic-background-backups" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Automatic Background Backups" href="#automatic-background-backups"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>File and ZIP creation runs outside the UI thread</li>
<li>Works while you use other applications</li>
<li>Automatic backups remain silent unless user interaction is required</li>
<li>Destination-conflict and retention confirmations can still require interaction</li>
<li>Optional minimize-to-system-tray behavior</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Status &amp; Feedback System</h3><a id="user-content-status--feedback-system" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Status &amp; Feedback System" href="#status--feedback-system"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Per-entry status indicator:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>🟢 OK</li>
<li>🟡 Warning</li>
<li>🔴 Error</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Hover for quick details:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Last backup time</li>
<li>Error messages</li>
<li>Skipped-file summary</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Click the info icon to open a copyable and scrollable details dialog</li>
<li>Skipped files are listed individually when available</li>
<li>Skipped files do not automatically mark a successful backup as failed</li>
<li>Retention is indicated by a red <code>R</code> marker</li>
<li>Source cleanup is indicated by a cyan <code>C</code> marker</li>
<li>Both markers are displayed when both features are active</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">ZIP Support</h3><a id="user-content-zip-support" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ZIP Support" href="#zip-support"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Optional compression into <code>.zip</code> archives</li>
<li>Uses the same versioning logic as folder-based backups</li>
<li>Temporary ZIP files are finalized only after successful creation</li>
<li>No additional metadata sidecar files are created</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Robust Error Handling</h3><a id="user-content-robust-error-handling" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Robust Error Handling" href="#robust-error-handling"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Option to ignore locked or in-use files</li>
<li>Backup can continue without interruption</li>
<li>Skipped files are reported per backup entry</li>
<li>Skipped-file summaries distinguish between files and folders</li>
<li>Failed or canceled backups do not trigger source cleanup</li>
<li>Useful for projects with temporary build files, IDE locks, or active services</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">System Tray Integration</h3><a id="user-content-system-tray-integration" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: System Tray Integration" href="#system-tray-integration"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">Minimize to tray</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Tray tooltip shows the next scheduled backup:</p>
<p dir="auto">Next backup in 2 minutes (19:52)</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Manual and automatic backups can show tray notifications</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Canceled backups are reported as canceled instead of completed</p>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Modernized UI</h3><a id="user-content-modernized-ui" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Modernized UI" href="#modernized-ui"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Clean, compact main window</li>
<li>Toolbar with quick actions:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Exit</li>
<li>Add path</li>
<li>Remove path</li>
<li>Settings</li>
<li>About</li>
<li>Backup</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Modern themed dialogs and scrollbars</li>
<li>Clear visual distinction between normal and destructive actions</li>
<li>Main window initially shows only a small number of rows</li>
<li>Window remains resizable</li>
<li>Resize indicator included</li>
<li>Tooltips and contextual help added for important controls</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🧩 How It Works</h2><a id="user-content--how-it-works" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🧩 How It Works" href="#-how-it-works"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Configure one or more <strong>source → target</strong> paths</li>
<li>Optionally define exclusions per backup pair</li>
<li>Choose a versioning method</li>
<li>Optionally configure:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Retention</li>
<li>Source cleanup</li>
<li>Custom backup timer</li>
<li>Error handling</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Click <strong>Backup</strong> or let the configured timer start it automatically</li>
<li>The app will:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Validate the source and target paths</li>
<li>Create a versioned directory or ZIP archive</li>
<li>Report progress directly in the backup-pair row</li>
<li>Skip problematic files if configured</li>
<li>Preview Source Cleanup and Retention candidates before deletion</li>
<li>Allow canceling the backup or continuing without deletion</li>
<li>Apply confirmed Source Cleanup only after a successful backup</li>
<li>Apply confirmed Retention after backup creation</li>
<li>Store the final backup status per entry</li>
<li>Persist the selected version only after success</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">⚙️ Settings Overview</h2><a id="user-content-️-settings-overview" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ⚙️ Settings Overview" href="#️-settings-overview"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Backup Timer</h3><a id="user-content-backup-timer" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Backup Timer" href="#backup-timer"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Flexible input:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>30s</code> → 30 seconds</li>
<li><code>5m</code> → 5 minutes</li>
<li><code>1h</code> → 1 hour</li>
<li><code>15</code> → 15 minutes by default</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Default Versioning</h3><a id="user-content-default-versioning" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Default Versioning" href="#default-versioning"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Starting version or pattern, for example:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>none</code></li>
<li><code>v1.0</code></li>
<li><code>1.0</code></li>
<li><code>yyyy-MM-dd</code></li>
<li><code>yyyyMMdd</code></li>
<li><code>yyyy-MM-dd-HH-mm</code></li>
<li><code>yyyyMMddHHmm</code></li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Auto Increment</h3><a id="user-content-auto-increment" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Auto Increment" href="#auto-increment"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Automatically increases compatible version numbers</li>
<li>Updated versions are saved only after a successful backup</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Retention</h3><a id="user-content-retention" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Retention" href="#retention"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Keep the latest configured number of backups</li>
<li>Keep backups for a configured number of days</li>
<li>Combine enabled rules with <code>AND</code> or <code>OR</code></li>
<li>Exclude tagged backups from deletion</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Source Cleanup</h3><a id="user-content-source-cleanup-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Source Cleanup" href="#source-cleanup-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Delete only explicitly configured file types</li>
<li>Restrict cleanup to specific source-relative folders</li>
<li>Keep the newest files or delete files older than a selected date</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Minimize to Systray</h3><a id="user-content-minimize-to-systray" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Minimize to Systray" href="#minimize-to-systray"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Keeps the app running quietly in the background</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Ignore Copy Errors</h3><a id="user-content-ignore-copy-errors" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Ignore Copy Errors" href="#ignore-copy-errors"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Skips locked or problematic files instead of stopping the backup</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">ZIP Backups</h3><a id="user-content-zip-backups" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: ZIP Backups" href="#zip-backups"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Enable to create one <code>.zip</code> archive per backup</li>
<li>Disable to create a versioned backup directory</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Per-Path Exclusions</h3><a id="user-content-per-path-exclusions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Per-Path Exclusions" href="#per-path-exclusions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Exclude folders or files from individual backup entries</li>
<li>Supports simple names, file extensions, relative paths, <code>*</code>, and <code>**</code></li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">🖥️ Runtime Requirement</h2><a id="user-content-️-runtime-requirement" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 🖥️ Runtime Requirement" href="#️-runtime-requirement"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">The published application targets Windows x64 and requires the .NET 8 Desktop Runtime because the project is framework-dependent (<code>SelfContained=false</code>).</p>
<p dir="auto">Application settings are stored in:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="&lt;application directory&gt;\Settings\EasyVersionBackup.settings.json"><pre class="notranslate"><code>&lt;application directory&gt;\Settings\EasyVersionBackup.settings.json
</code></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The application no longer creates <code>.evbmeta</code> sidecar files beside ZIP backups.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">💡 Use Cases</h2><a id="user-content--use-cases" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 💡 Use Cases" href="#-use-cases"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Backup Visual Studio projects</li>
<li>Create safe snapshots before refactoring</li>
<li>Preserve game saves while automatically removing older save files</li>
<li>Keep only a defined number of recent backups</li>
<li>Protect release backups using retention tags</li>
<li>Save versions before deployments or releases</li>
<li>Run automatic backups while working</li>
</ul>

    <style>
      .user-mention[href$="/UncleRiot"] {
        color: var(--color-user-mention-fg);
        background-color: var(--bgColor-attention-muted, var(--color-attention-subtle));
        border-radius: 2px;
        margin-left: -2px;
        margin-right: -2px;
      }
      .user-mention[href$="/UncleRiot"]:before,
      .user-mention[href$="/UncleRiot"]:after {
        content: '';
        display: inline-block;
        width: 2px;
      }
